Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- kubeflow is a Kubernetes component.
- deploying 9 workload(s) (controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, deployment, deployment, deployment, manager, manager) with 10 container(s).
- watching 12 resource type(s).
- exposing 3 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8443.
- with 2 webhook(s) (1 validating, 1 mutating).
- protected by 2 network policy/policies.
- connecting to external services\: requests.
- runtime dependencies\: MLflow, image-registry.
- with 157 operator config constants (2 image references, 27 env vars).
- exposing 5 Prometheus metrics.
- integrates with Istio, Prometheus, management relationship via labels/annotations.
- deploys kube-rbac-proxy sidecar for RBAC-based authorization.
